# KnowBe4 Security Inspector

Portable contract for KnowBe4 phishing, training, user-risk, account-governance, and optional PhishER assessments.

## Purpose

Assess KnowBe4 phishing, training, user-risk, and account-governance posture with optional PhishER enrichment.

## Design guidance

Use complete active-user, enrollment, campaign, test, and recipient populations for rates and joins. PhishER is independently licensed and authenticated; not configured, denied, truncated, and failed child reads remain explicit and cannot prove compliance.

## Shared integration contract

This specification requires [shared integration contract version 1.1](./integration-contract.md). The raw contract is available at https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ethanolivertroy/grclanker/main/specs/integration-contract.md.

## Known runtime gaps

- Reporting API Link rel=next pagination is exhausted before population metrics are calculated; per-test recipients preserve individual read failures and sample caps.
- PhishER is optional and independently authenticated; not configured is retained as a named collection state and cannot be mistaken for an empty message or rule inventory.
- PII redaction changes exported labels only after counts and joins are computed from stable vendor identifiers.
- The Reporting API exposes neither USB drop campaign execution nor a complete administrative-role directory on every subscription; unavailable fields remain manual.
- KNOWBE4-10 can pass when the security-test collection is empty but retained recipient samples show no remediation is due or at least 90 percent remediation even though recipient_reads_complete is false; unread recipients are not reflected.
- KNOWBE4-18 can pass when the security-test collection is empty, activity metadata marks the read partial, users_without_loaded_activity is zero, and inactive_users is null; unobserved activity is not reflected.

## Tools

| Tool | Purpose | Finding IDs | Result shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| `knowbe4_check_access` | Validate read-only KnowBe4 Reporting API access across account, users, groups, phishing campaigns, security tests, training campaigns, enrollments, store purchases, and policies, plus optional PhishER GraphQL access. | None | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `knowbe4_assess_phishing_program` | Assess the KnowBe4 phishing simulation program: test cadence, active-user coverage, phish-prone percentage, failure-rate trend, campaign targeting, Phish Alert Button report rate (with optional PhishER enrichment), and scheduling regularity (spec controls 1, 2, 6, 7, 9, 19, 20). | `KNOWBE4-01`, `KNOWBE4-02`, `KNOWBE4-06`, `KNOWBE4-07`, `KNOWBE4-09`, `KNOWBE4-19`, `KNOWBE4-20` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `knowbe4_assess_training_program` | Assess the KnowBe4 security awareness training program: campaign completion rates, new-user enrollment timeliness, remedial training after phishing failures, training content currency, and required compliance modules (spec controls 3, 4, 10, 11, 17). | `KNOWBE4-03`, `KNOWBE4-04`, `KNOWBE4-10`, `KNOWBE4-11`, `KNOWBE4-17` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `knowbe4_assess_user_risk` | Assess KnowBe4 user risk and hygiene: organization risk score distribution, group coverage across phishing and training campaigns, and inactive users who have not participated in any campaign (spec controls 5, 8, 18). | `KNOWBE4-05`, `KNOWBE4-08`, `KNOWBE4-18` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `knowbe4_assess_account_governance` | Assess KnowBe4 account governance: console administrator count and domains, SSO enforcement (manual evidence), report review cadence (manual evidence), USB drop tests (manual evidence), and callback voice-phishing tests (spec controls 12, 13, 14, 15, 16). | `KNOWBE4-12`, `KNOWBE4-13`, `KNOWBE4-14`, `KNOWBE4-15`, `KNOWBE4-16` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `knowbe4_export_audit_bundle` | Export a KnowBe4 audit package covering all 20 spec controls: raw Reporting API snapshots in core_data/, normalized findings and control coverage in analysis/, executive summary, unified matrix, and per-framework reports in compliance/, QUICK_REFERENCE.md, an _errors.log when collection is partial, and a zip archive. | `KNOWBE4-01`, `KNOWBE4-02`, `KNOWBE4-03`, `KNOWBE4-04`, `KNOWBE4-05`, `KNOWBE4-06`, `KNOWBE4-07`, `KNOWBE4-08`, `KNOWBE4-09`, `KNOWBE4-10`, `KNOWBE4-11`, `KNOWBE4-12`, `KNOWBE4-13`, `KNOWBE4-14`, `KNOWBE4-15`, `KNOWBE4-16`, `KNOWBE4-17`, `KNOWBE4-18`, `KNOWBE4-19`, `KNOWBE4-20` | A text result plus output directory, paired archive path, file count, finding count, and collection-error count. |

### Parameters

#### `knowbe4_check_access`

| Parameter | Kind | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `api_token` | string | no | KnowBe4 Reporting API key. Defaults to KNOWBE4_API_TOKEN, then api_token in ~/.knowbe4-inspector/config.yaml. |
| `region` | string or string or string or string or string | no | KnowBe4 hosting region that selects the API base URL. Defaults to KNOWBE4_REGION, then the config file, then us. |
| `base_url` | string | no | Override for the Reporting API base URL (normally derived from region, for example https://us.api.knowbe4.com). Defaults to KNOWBE4_BASE_URL. |
| `phisher_api_token` | string | no | Optional PhishER Product API key for GraphQL enrichment. Defaults to KNOWBE4_PHISHER_API_TOKEN, then phisher_api_token in the config file. |
| `phisher_graphql_url` | string | no | Override for the PhishER GraphQL endpoint (normally derived from region, for example https://training.knowbe4.com/graphql). Defaults to KNOWBE4_PHISHER_GRAPHQL_URL. |
| `config_file` | string | no | Path to a YAML config file with api_token, region, and optional phisher_api_token. Defaults to KNOWBE4_CONFIG_FILE or ~/.knowbe4-inspector/config.yaml. |
| `timeout_seconds` | number | no | HTTP timeout in seconds. Defaults to 30. |
| `redact_pii` | boolean | no | Replace user emails and names with stable pseudonyms in findings and exported data. Defaults to KNOWBE4_REDACT_PII or false. |

## Authentication

Supported modes:

- KnowBe4 Reporting API bearer token
- Optional independent PhishER Product API bearer token

Credential precedence, highest first:

1. Explicit tool arguments
2. KNOWBE4_* environment variables
3. Explicit or default KnowBe4 YAML config

Environment variables: `KNOWBE4_CONFIG_FILE`, `KNOWBE4_API_TOKEN`, `KNOWBE4_REGION`, `KNOWBE4_BASE_URL`, `KNOWBE4_PHISHER_API_TOKEN`, `KNOWBE4_PHISHER_GRAPHQL_URL`, `KNOWBE4_TIMEOUT`, `KNOWBE4_REDACT_PII`

Configuration locations: ~/.knowbe4-inspector/config.yaml, Explicit path from config_file or KNOWBE4_CONFIG_FILE

Credential and deployment variants: us, eu, ca, uk, de, Optional region-specific PhishER GraphQL endpoint

Configuration fields: `api_token`, `region`, `base_url`, `phisher_api_token`, `phisher_graphql_url`, `timeout`, `redact_pii`

Malformed configuration: Reject malformed or ambiguous configuration before any request; never echo credential values.

## Permissions

| Kind | Permission, role, or plan | Unlocks | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| role | `KnowBe4 Reporting API read access` | `account`, `risk-history`, `users`, `groups`, `group-members`, `phishing-campaigns`, `security-tests`, `security-test-recipients`, `callback-tests`, `training-campaigns`, `training-enrollments`, `store-purchases`, `training-policies` |  |
| license | `PhishER subscription and Product API token` | `phisher-messages`, `phisher-rules` |  |

## Pagination

| Surfaces | Cursor or marker | Page size | Item cap | Page cap | Total semantics | Stop conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `risk-history`, `users`, `groups`, `group-members`, `phishing-campaigns`, `security-tests`, `security-test-recipients`, `callback-tests`, `training-campaigns`, `training-enrollments`, `store-purchases`, `training-policies` | `Link: rel=next`, `page`, `per_page` | 500 | 20000 | none | Completion requires the same-origin Link rel=next chain to end before the inventory-specific item cap. | No rel=next link; Inventory item cap; Repeated next link; Off-origin next link; Empty page with next link |
| `phisher-messages`, `phisher-rules` | `pagination.page`, `pagination.pages`, `pagination.totalCount`, `pagination.nextPageKey` | 100 | 1000 | none | Completion requires pages and totalCount to be accounted for and nextPageKey to advance until absent. | Last page; Reported total reached; Configured item cap; Repeated nextPageKey; Empty page before reported total |

## Rate limits

| Scope | Documented limit | Retry headers | Retryable statuses | Policy |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| KnowBe4 Security Inspector | Reporting API daily request quota varies by subscription tier; the runtime does not assume a fixed remaining quota. | `Retry-After`, `X-RateLimit-Remaining`, `X-RateLimit-Reset` | 429, 500, 502, 503, 504 | Retry three times, honoring Retry-After up to 30 seconds and otherwise using exponential backoff from one second. |

### Ordered decision rules

Rules are evaluated from lowest order number to highest. The first matching condition determines the finding status; later rules are not evaluated.

A `matches` condition performs a regular-expression search; anchors are required for whole-value matching, and an `i` flag requests case-insensitive matching. A `ratio` condition divides the numerator by the denominator, applies the declared scale, and rounds to the declared decimal places by choosing the nearest value with exact half cases rounded toward positive infinity; a zero, null, or missing denominator does not match.

## Collection states

| State | Required rendering |
|---|---|
| complete | complete: proven API exhaustion or a successful single-object read. |
| truncated | truncated: preserve seen and total when available plus the exact stop reason. |
| unreadable | unreadable: render data and counts as null and retain a scrubbed error envelope. |
| denied | denied: render null evidence with the endpoint and HTTP status, never an empty inventory. |
| not requested | not_requested: identify the unreadable parent dependency and do not invent an HTTP status. |
| not configured | not_configured: identify the absent optional feature or credential without treating it as compliant. |

## Integration-specific scrubbing

Shared contract version: 1.1.

Projection stage: Project records to verdict-consumed fields, scrub configured and discovered credentials, then scrub again at every report and archive write sink.

Sensitive fields and values: api_token, phisher_api_token, authorization, cookie, email, first_name, last_name, manager_name, ip_address

Credential formats: KnowBe4 Reporting API bearer tokens, PhishER Product API bearer tokens, authorization headers, user PII

Reviewed benign exceptions: Stable non-secret resource identifiers and public documentation URLs remain visible unless carried in a credential field.

Integration-specific rules:

- Withhold undocumented error bodies; retain only status, media type, byte length, and allowlisted vendor error codes.
- Remove URL user information, queries, and fragments from evidence and reject off-origin pagination links.
- Unavailable counts, arrays, maps, and negative flags are null rather than fabricated empty values.

Projected fields by surface:

| Surface | Allowed fields |
|---|---|
| `account` | `name`, `subscription_level`, `number_of_seats`, `current_risk_score`, `sso_enabled`, `admins` |
| `risk-history` | `date`, `risk_score` |
| `users` | `id`, `email`, `status`, `risk_score`, `phish_prone_percentage`, `created_at`, `last_login` |
| `groups` | `id`, `name`, `member_count` |
| `group-members` | `id`, `email`, `status` |
| `phishing-campaigns` | `campaign_id`, `name`, `status`, `start_date`, `frequency`, `groups` |
| `security-tests` | `pst_id`, `campaign_id`, `started_at`, `status`, `phish_prone_percentage` |
| `security-test-recipients` | `user_id`, `sent_at`, `opened_at`, `clicked_at`, `reported_at`, `status` |
| `callback-tests` | `pst_id`, `campaign_id`, `started_at`, `status` |
| `training-campaigns` | `campaign_id`, `name`, `status`, `start_date`, `end_date`, `groups` |
| `training-enrollments` | `enrollment_id`, `user_id`, `campaign_id`, `store_purchase_id`, `status`, `enrollment_date`, `completion_date` |
| `store-purchases` | `store_purchase_id`, `name`, `publisher`, `published_at`, `topics` |
| `training-policies` | `id`, `name`, `trigger`, `remedial_training`, `active` |
| `phisher-messages` | `id`, `receivedAt`, `classification`, `reportedBy`, `status` |
| `phisher-rules` | `id`, `name`, `active`, `action` |

### Record schemas

#### finding

- `id`
- `title`
- `severity`
- `status`
- `summary`
- `evidence`
- `framework mappings`

#### collection_marker

- `collected`
- `status`
- `endpoint`
- `error`

#### bundle_result

- `outputDir`
- `zipPath`
- `fileCount`
- `findingCount`
- `errorCount`

#### assessment

- `title or category`
- `summary`
- `findings`
- `errors when collection was partial`

#### pagination_state

- `items or rows seen`
- `reported total when available`
- `pages`
- `truncated`
- `stop reason`

JSON formatting: UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline.

Overwrite policy: Allocate knowbe4-audit-<UTC timestamp> and add a numeric suffix when the directory or archive exists.

Path safety: Resolve beneath the configured output root and reject traversal, unsafe parents, files, and symbolic-link escapes.

Archive pairing: Create <allocated-directory>.zip beside the allocated KnowBe4 audit directory.
